FROM [Items] AS [i]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM OPENJSON(@__Select_0) WITH ([value] nvarchar(max) '$') AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[value] = UPPER([i].[Role]))
ORDER BY (
    SELECT TOP(1) [t].[GregorianDate]
    FROM [TransactionEntries] AS [t]
    WHERE [i].[ItemId] = [t].[ItemId]
    ORDER BY [t].[GregorianDate] DESC) DESC"
